- **weread**: Use this skill whenever the user mentions 微信读书, WeRead, 书架, 读书时间, 读书笔记, 划线, or wants to do anything with their personal reading data. - **weread-digest**: Use when a book was read in 微信读书 but has no 读书笔记 in the local knowledge base, or when a 微信读书 book needs to become knowledge-base notes and X 推文. - **weread-socrates**: 微信读书苏格拉底伴读（纯离线单文件）——用提问把你还回原文，而不是替你总结；Reader Memory v2 记住你读到哪、想过什么；跨书关系只在你确认后才建立。想把一本书读成自己的、想被追问而不是被概括、想做跨书观点对照时用我。不抓取微信读书正文、不联网、不需要账号或 Cookie，只读你主动提供的内容；不替代原书阅读。Offline single-file Socratic reading companion for WeRead — question-led reading instead of summaries, Reader Memory v2 that remembers where you are and what you thought, cross-book links created only after your confirmation. Use when you want to read a book as your own, be questioned rather than summarized, or compare ideas across books. No scraping, no network, no account or cookies; uses only content you provide.
